Let's read the previous state of art text again carefully:
"Starting from a nonnegative step function f=(a_0,...,a_{n-1}) normalized so that sum_j a_j = sqrt(2n), set M=||f*f||∞. Next compute g_0=(b_0,...,b_{n-1}) by solving a linear program, i.e. maximizing sum_j b_j subject to b_j≥0 and ||f*g_0||∞≤M; as is standard, the optimum is attained at an extreme point determined by an active set of binding inequalities, here corresponding to important constraints where the convolution bound (f*g_0)(x)≤M is tight and limiting. Rescale g_0 to match normalization, g=..., and update f←(1-t)f+t g for small t>0. Repeating this step produces a sequence with nonincreasing ||f*f||∞, and the iteration is continued until it stabilizes."

This is not standard for autocorr minimization? Let's see: For fixed f and target M, find g such that cross-convolution f*g ≤ M. Then update f. This might minimize `||f*f||∞` by ensuring near active set? Suppose at optimum f*f=M active. If we move to g, cross with f ≤M, then for small t, (f+t(g-f))*(f+t(g-f)) = f*f + t(f*g + g*f -2f*f) +... If f*g ≤M and f*f has max M, this may not decrease max. Hmm.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s-10
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # obtain n and base
    try:
       base=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except: base=np.ones(1)
    base=np.clip(base,0,1000)
    n=int(base.size)
    if n<1: base=np.ones(1); n=1
    # candidate pool
    def make_list(x): return [float(y) for y in np.asarray(x).ravel()]
    def safe_eval(x):
       try: v=evaluate_sequence(x.tolist()); return v if np.isfinite(v) else np.inf
       except: return np.inf
    def norm1(x): s=x.sum(); if s>0: return x/s; return x
    candidates=[]
    # uniform
    uniform=np.ones(n)/n
    # parent normalized? raw parent maybe sum not 1; scale invariant but cap may affect. Include raw clipped and normalized.
    if base.sum()>0: candidates.append(base)
    # maybe parent smoothed? no
    # one_hot peak maybe at index with max base? If actual no0, best; if full, high.
    one=np.zeros(n); one[0]=1.0
    candidates.append(one)
    # alternate? maybe not.
    # low-density random support p=0.75? Could be good for no0 target and full score moderate.
    # But if n large, evaluating random candidate takes conv O(n^2) if using evaluate_sequence (uses np.convolve) expensive if many. We can limit.
```

Implement `conv_sq(a, N=None)`:
```
def _nextpow2(x): return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()
Nconv_cache = _nextpow2(2*n-1)
def autocorr(a):
    Fa=np.fft.rfft(a,Nconv)
    b=np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fa,Nconv)[:2*n-1]
    return b
```

Let's design no0 search algorithm in detail:
```
def optimize_no0(a0, budget_left):
    a = normalize(a0)
    best = a.copy(); best_s = score_no0(a)
    actual_best = ...
    Nconv = nextpow2(2*n-1)
    eta = 0.5
    top_frac = 0.05
    patience = 0
    it=0
    while time<deadline:
       b = autocorr(a)
       bpos = b[n:] # length n-1, lags 1..n-1
       M = float(bpos.max())
       if M <= 0: break
       # active weights
       # top count: min(n-1, max(50, int(top_frac*(n-1))))
       k = min(n-1, max(30, int(top_frac*(n-1))))
       idx = bpos.argpartition(-k)[-k:]
       topb = bpos[idx]
       # beta = 50/M maybe if M>0
       beta = 50.0 / M
       # stable softmax of beta*(topb - Mtop) where Mtop=topb.max()
       z = beta*(topb - topb.max())
       p = np.exp(z); p/=p.sum()
       # optionally add small background over all lags to avoid ignoring growth: p_all = (1-mix)*p_top + mix/(n-1) maybe mix=0.05
       # Build list of (ell, weight) where ell=idxrank+1.
       # Limit weights > 1e-6
       active = [(i+1, p[i]) for i in np.argsort(p) if p[i] > 1e-8]  # careful idx mapping
       # gradient direct
       grad = np.zeros(n)
       for ell,w in active:
           # if w too small skip
           sh1 = np.zeros(n); sh1[ell:] = a[:-ell]; sh2 = np.zeros(n); sh2[:-ell] = a[ell:]
           grad += w*(sh1+sh2)
       scale = max(M, 1e-18)
       # line search: try eta values
       base_score = score_no0(a) (we have M -> 2*n*M)
       best_new = a; best_new_s=base_score
       for eta_try in [eta*0.25, eta*0.5, eta, eta*2, eta*4]:
           u = -eta_try * grad / scale
           # avoid underflow: clip u > -50
           u = np.clip(u, -50, 50)
           cand = a * np.exp(u)
           # optional floor min to preserve positivity? Use floor 0
           s = cand.sum()
           if s <=0: continue
           cand /= s
           sc = 2*n*max_no0(autocorr(cand))
           if sc < best_new_s:
               best_new_s=sc; best_new=cand
       if best_new_s < best_s - 1e-12:
           a=best_new; best_s=best_new_s; best=best_new
           patience=0
           # occasionally check actual evaluator
           if it%20==0:
               act = safe_eval(a.tolist())
               if act < global_actual_best: update global
       else:
           patience +=1
           # perturb or adjust
           if patience >= 10:
               # local random multiplicative perturbation
               p_norm = int(n*0.1)
               idx = rng.choice(n, p_norm, replace=False)
               a[idx] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.2, p_norm))
               a = np.clip(a,0,1000); normalize
               patience=0
               top_frac = max(0.02, top_frac*0.8) or *1.2
               eta = min(3.0, eta*1.2)
       it+=1
       # anneal
       if it%100==0: top_frac = min(0.3, top_frac*1.05); eta = max(0.05, eta*0.98)
       return best
```

Pseudo:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+max(1,budget_s)-10
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    try: base=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,float)
    except: base=np.ones(4498)
    base=np.clip(base,0,1000)
    n=base.size
    if n<2: return [1.0]
    Nconv=1 << ((2*n-1)-1).bit_length()
    def conv(a):
        F=np.fft.rfft(a,Nconv)
        return np.fft.irfft(F*F,Nconv)[:2*n-1]
    def score_arr(a):
       S=a.sum(); if S<0.01: return inf
       c=conv(a); return 2*n*c.max()/S*S
    # normalize helper
```
But calling `evaluate_sequence` for exact uses direct conv; we can use our `score_arr` for speed (same if no clipping? We keep clip). For final validation, evaluate.

Local optimizer:
Input a (sum1). Use actual score `score_arr` (surrogate) but update best exact periodic.
Parameters:
- `best_a`, `best_s` (surrogate), `actual_best_a`, `actual_best_s` from evaluate_sequence.
- We'll run for a certain time (e.g., 600s) or until no improvement. Since target maybe reachable, enough.
- At each phase, maybe change `top_frac`, `beta`, `eta`.
Pseudo loop:
```
top_frac = 0.10
beta_mult = 50.0
eta = 0.5
patience=0
it=0
while time < deadline:
   c=conv(a); M=c.max(); if not finite break
   # weight selection
   L=2*n-1
   # sort indices by c
   # To avoid sorting 8995 each iter, can use argpartition top.
   k = max(20, int(top_frac*L))
   idx = c.argpartition(-k)[-k:]
   # within top, softmax beta*(c[idx] - c[idx].max())
   btop=c[idx]; z=(btop-btop.max())* (beta_mult/M)
   p=np.exp(z); p/=p.sum()
   # optional add small global: p_all = (1-mix)*p over idx + mix/L all
   mix = 0.02 maybe
   w=np.zeros(L)
   w[idx]=p
   if mix>0: w += mix/L; w[idx]*=(1-mix); # renormalize? sum top p=(1-mix), all mix/L sum=mix
   # gradient via FFT
   ar=a[::-1]
   Wf=np.fft.rfft(w,Ngrad)
   Af=np.fft.rfft(ar,Ngrad)
   V=np.fft.irfft(Wf*Af,Ngrad)[n-1:n-1+n]
   grad=2*V
   # line search: scale=M
   base_s=M*2*n? since sum1. Use cmax=M.
   eta_vals = [eta*0.25, eta*0.5, eta, eta*2, eta*4]
   best_cand=None; best_MM=M
   for e in eta_vals:
       u=-e*grad/M
       u=np.clip(u, -50, 50)
       cand=a*np.exp(u)
       s=cand.sum()
       if s<=0: continue
       cand/=s
       cm=conv(cand).max()
       if cm<best_MM:
           best_MM=cm; best_cand=cand
   if best_MM < M - 1e-15:
       a=best_cand; M=best_MM; patience=0; # update best surrogate
       # update actual best every few iters
       if it%10==0:
           sc=evaluate_sequence(a.tolist())
           if sc<actual_best_s: update
   else:
       patience+=1
       if patience>5:
           # perturb
           # random multiplicative or Gaussian on log
           scale = 0.1 if patience<20 else 0.25
           a *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, scale, n))
           a=np.clip(a,0,1000); normalize
           # maybe try reverse blend
           blend=rng.uniform(0,0.3)
           a=(1-blend)*a+blend*a[::-1]; normalize
           patience=0
           # occasionally change params
           top_frac = np.clip(top_frac*(1 if M>prev else 0.9),0.02,0.5)
           beta_mult = np.clip(beta_mult*1.1,10,500)
           eta = min(2.0, eta*1.2) if patience? 
   it+=1
   if it%50==0: adapt...
```

Function:
```
def random_old(n, rng, alpha):
    f = rng.normal(size=n//2+1)
    # taper high freq
    taper = 1.0/(1.0+np.linspace(0,3,n//2+1)**2)
    f *= taper; f[0]=0 maybe; complex = f + 1j*rng.normal...; x=irfft...
    x -= x.mean(); x = x/(std+eps)
    a = 1 + alpha*x; a=np.maximum(a,1e-8); normalize
```

Weight computation:
```
def weights_from_c(c, M, beta, mix):
   z = beta * (c - M)
   # max subtraction already by M? c-M ≤0, min -βM maybe -50. If beta=50/M, okay.
   p = np.exp(z)
   p_sum = p.sum()
   if not finite or p_sum<=0: p=np.ones(L)/L
   else: p /= p_sum
   if mix>0:
       p = (1-mix)*p + mix/L
       # sum remains 1 since uniform sum 1
   return p
```
If beta huge and min -100 exp underflows; p only max. Fine.

Line search:
```
def line_search(a, grad, M, etas):
    best=a; bestM=M
    for e in etas:
       u=-e*grad/M
       u=np.clip(u, -40, 40)
       cand=a*np.exp(u)
       cand=np.maximum(cand, 1e-300)
       s=cand.sum()
       if s<=0: continue
       cand/=s
       c=conv_fast(cand)
       cm=float(c.max())
       if cm<bestM:
           bestM=cm; best=cand
    return best,bestM
```
If grad all zeros (M maybe 0), no change.

```
def perturb(a, scale):
   a2=a*np.exp(rng.normal(0,scale,n))
   a2=np.clip(a2,0,1000); normalize
   # optional reverse blend
   if rng.random()<0.3:
      bl=rng.uniform(0,0.4); a2=(1-bl)*a2+bl*a[::-1]; normalize
   return a2
```

Pseudo code final:
```
import numpy as np, time, math

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 15.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    try:
        base_in = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except NameError:
        base_in = np.ones(4498, dtype=np.float64)
    base_in = np.clip(base_in, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = int(base_in.size)
    if n <= 0:
        n = 4498; base_in = np.ones(n)
    if n == 1:
        return [1.0]

    L = 2*n - 1
    Nconv = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    Ngrad = 1 << (3*n - 2 - 1).bit_length()

    def conv_fast(arr):
        F = np.fft.rfft(arr, Nconv)
        return np.fft.irfft(F*F, Nconv)[:L]

    def norm1(arr):
        a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = a.sum()
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
            return None
        return a / s

    def surrogate_score(a):
        c = conv_fast(a)
        m = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        return (2.0*n*m) if np.isfinite(m) else float('inf')

    def exact_score(a):
        try:
            vals = [float(x) for x in a]
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(vals))
            return sc if np.isfinite(sc) else float('inf')
        except Exception:
            return float('inf')

    # candidate generation and select best surrogate/exact
    candidates=[]
    def add(arr):
       a=norm1(arr); if a is not None: candidates.append(a)
    # parent
    if base_in.sum()>0: add(base_in)
    # uniform
    add(np.ones(n))
    # param linear centered
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    for alpha in (0.4,0.5,0.6,0.7,1.0):
       arr=1.0+alpha*(2.0*x-1.0)
       if arr.min()>=-1e-12: add(np.maximum(arr,0.0))
       arr=1.0-alpha*(2.0*x-1.0) # same reversed
    # exponential
    for beta in (1.0,2.0):
       arr=np.exp(beta*(2.0*x-1.0)); add(arr)
    # random smooth
    for _ in range(8):
       if n>=64:
          m=n//2+1
          sp=np.zeros(m,complex)
          for i in range(m):
             taper=1.0/(1.0+ (i/ max(1,n//10))**2) # maybe
          # simpler: noise = rng.normal(n); taper via FFT?
```

noise=np.convolve(noise,kernel,'same')
noise -= noise.mean(); noise /= (noise.std()+1e-9)
for alpha in [0.2,0.5]: add(np.maximum(1+alpha*noise,1e-6))
```

Let's check gradient alignment one more time with a simple case to avoid wrong update. n=2, a=[0.5,0.5], conv [0.25,0.5,0.25], max M=0.5 at k=1. Weights w=[0,1,0] (sum1). grad_j=2∑w_k a_{k-j}=2a_{1-j} valid: j=0 a1=0.5 ->1, j=1 a0=0.5 ->1, uniform. Our FFT with p=[0,1,0], ar=[0.5,0.5], conv p*ar = [0,0.5,0.5,0]? indices n-1=1..2 => [0.5,0.5], grad=[1,1]. Multiplicative no change. Uniform score2.
If a=[0.9,0.1], conv [0.81,0.18,0.01], M=0.81 at k=0. w=[1,0,0]. grad_j=2a_{0-j} valid only j=0 ->1.8. Update reduces a0, increases a1, should lower max? New a maybe flatter; uniform score 2 but current score 2*2*0.81=3.24, okay. Our FFT p=[1,0,0], ar=[0.1,0.9], conv=[0.1,0.9,0,0]? index1=0.9? Wait n=2, p*ar full: [1*0.1=0.1, 1*0.9=0.9,0,0]; slice index1..2 => [0.9,0]; grad [1.8,0]. Good.
So gradient correct.

Candidate generation random smooth: Use `np.convolve` direct with kernel length w; for n=4498 w~90, okay. But to avoid O(n^2) if w large, w = max(3, n//100) ~45; fine.
```
def random_smooth(alpha):
    noise = rng.normal(size=n)
    w = max(5, n//80)
    r = np.arange(-w,w+1)
    kernel = np.exp(-0.5*(r/(max(1,w//3)))**2)
    kernel /= kernel.sum()
    noise = np.convolve(noise, kernel, mode='same')
    std = noise.std()
    if std < 1e-12: return None
    noise = (noise - noise.mean())/std
    arr = 1.0 + alpha*noise
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 1e-6)
    return arr
```
This random has mean1, positive if alpha small. Generate alpha [0.3,0.6]. 4 candidates.

    ap = base_arr * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(scale), n))
    if rng.random() < 0.25:

    s=ap.sum()

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 15.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Base length and initialization
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    try:
        base_in = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        base_in = np.ones(4498, dtype=np.float64)

    base_in = np.clip(base_in, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = int(base_in.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return [1.0]

    L = 2 * n - 1
    Nconv = 1 << int(L - 1).bit_length()
    Ngrad = 1 << int(3 * n - 2 - 1).bit_length()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def norm1(arr):
        a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
            return None
        return a / s

    def conv_fast(a):
        F = np.fft.rfft(a, Nconv)
        return np.fft.irfft(F * F, Nconv)[:L]

    def surrogate_score(a):
        c = conv_fast(a)
        m = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(m):
            return float("inf")
        return 2.0 * n * m

    def exact_score(a):
        try:
            vals = [float(x) for x in a]
            s = float(evaluate_sequence(vals))
            return s if np.isfinite(s) else float("inf")
        except Exception:
            return float("inf")

    def make_list(a):
        return [float(x) for x in np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)]

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Build a pool of starting points.
    # The supplied parent is used if present; other simple smooth
    # constructions are used as restarts / diversity generators.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    cand_list = []

    def add_candidate(arr):
        a = norm1(arr)
        if a is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            cand_list.append(a)

    if base_in.sum() > 1e-12:
        add_candidate(base_in)
    add_candidate(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)

    # Linear centered variations (reversal symmetric cases are harmless).
    for alpha in (0.35, 0.5, 0.65, 0.8, 1.0):
        arr = 1.0 + alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0)
        add_candidate(np.maximum(arr, 0.0))

    # Exponential tilts.
    for beta in (1.0, 1.8, 3.0):
        arr = np.exp(beta * (2.0 * x - 1.0))
        add_candidate(arr)

    # Smooth random perturbations around a constant.
    w = max(5, n // 80)
    r = np.arange(-w, w + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    kernel = np.exp(-0.5 * (r / max(1.0, w / 3.0)) ** 2)
    kernel /= kernel.sum()

    for alpha in (0.25, 0.45, 0.70):
        noise = rng.normal(size=n)
        noise = np.convolve(noise, kernel, mode="same")
        std = float(noise.std())
        if std <= 1e-12:
            continue
        noise = (noise - float(noise.mean())) / std
        arr = 1.0 + alpha * noise
        add_candidate(np.maximum(arr, 1e-6))

    # Keep the pool trimmed.
    scored = []
    for a in cand_list:
        s = surrogate_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            scored.append((s, a))
    scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    cand_pool = [a for _, a in scored[:12]]
    if not cand_pool:
        cand_pool = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]

    # Choose best starter by both surrogate and exact evaluator.
    best_s = float("inf")
    best_a = cand_pool[0].copy()
    best_es = float("inf")
    best_exact = best_a.copy()

    # Exact score the most promising starts (bounded work).
    exact_evals = 0
    max_initial_exact = 10 if budget_s >= 300 else 4
    for a in cand_pool:
        s = surrogate_score(a)
        if s < best_s:
            best_s = s
            best_a = a.copy()

        if exact_evals < max_initial_exact:
            ex = exact_score(a)
            exact_evals += 1
            if ex < best_es:
                best_es = ex
                best_exact = a.copy()

    # Make sure the overall surrogate best has an exact score.
    ex = exact_score(best_a)
    if ex < best_es:
        best_es = ex
        best_exact = best_a.copy()

    if best_es == float("inf"):
        best_exact = best_a.copy()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Local search: multiplicative descent on a smoothed maximum of
    # the convolution coefficients, with exact line-search checks.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    a = best_a.copy()
    current_score = surrogate_score(a)

    if not np.isfinite(current_score) or current_score >= 2.5:
        # If something went wrong with starters, fall back to uniform.
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        current_score = 2.0

    beta_mult = 60.0
    mix = 0.05
    eta = 0.8
    patience = 0
    it = 0

    def gradient_from_weights(w):
        # w length L, sum 1. grad_j = 2 sum_{k} w_k a_{k-j}, valid.
        ar = a[::-1]
        Wf = np.fft.rfft(w, Ngrad)
        Af = np.fft.rfft(ar, Ngrad)
        V = np.fft.irfft(Wf * Af, Ngrad)
        return 2.0 * V[n - 1:n - 1 + n]

    def perturb_array(base, scale):
        ap = base * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(scale), n))
        if rng.random() < 0.30:
            bl = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.35))
            ap = (1.0 - bl) * ap + bl * base[::-1]
        ap = np.clip(ap, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(ap.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
            return None
        return ap / s

    def blend_with_pool(base, current_score_local):
        non_accept_limit = 2.0
        if np.isfinite(best_es):
            non_accept_limit = min(2.0, float(best_es) + 0.18)

        choices = []
        for _ in range(6):
            b = cand_pool[int(rng.integers(0, len(cand_pool)))]
            u = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.85))
            ar = (1.0 - u) * base + u * b
            ar = np.clip(ar, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s = float(ar.sum())
            if s <= 1e-12:
                continue
            ar /= s
            sc = surrogate_score(ar)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                choices.append((sc, ar))

        if not choices:
            return base, current_score_local, False

        choices.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
        sc, ar = choices[0]
        accept = False
        if sc < current_score_local - 1e-12:
            accept = True
        elif patience >= 25 and sc < non_accept_limit:
            accept = True

        if accept:
            return ar, sc, True
        return base, current_score_local, False

    # Main loop.
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 12.0:
        it += 1

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            a = best_a.copy()
            current_score = surrogate_score(a)
            continue

        c = conv_fast(a)
        M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        current_score = 2.0 * n * M

        # Focus weights on near-maximum coefficients, but keep enough
        # diffusion to move off plateau/stationary points.
        scaleM = max(M, 1e-18)
        z = (beta_mult / scaleM) * (c - M)
        z = np.clip(z, -40.0, 0.0)
        p = np.exp(z)
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ps) or ps <= 1e-300:
            p = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        else:
            p = p / ps

        if mix > 0.0:
            p = (1.0 - mix) * p + (mix / float(L))
            ps = float(p.sum())
            if ps > 0.0 and np.isfinite(ps):
                p = p / ps

        g = gradient_from_weights(p)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)) or float(g.sum()) <= 1e-300:
            # Bad gradient: perturb and retry.
            ap = perturb_array(a, 0.25)
            if ap is not None:
                a = ap
                current_score = surrogate_score(a)
            patience += 1
            continue

        # Line search over several mirror-descent steps.
        eta_candidates = [0.08, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.2, 4.5]
        # Bias the search around the currently preferred eta.
        eta_candidates = [eta * v for v in (0.4, 0.7, 1.0, 1.6, 2.6, 4.0)]
        eta_candidates = sorted(set(float(max(1e-4, e)) for e in eta_candidates))[:6]

        best_step_score = current_score
        best_step_arr = None
        best_step_eta = eta

        for e in eta_candidates:
            u = -e * g / scaleM
            u = np.clip(u, -35.0, 35.0)
            cand = a * np.exp(u)
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-300)
            s = float(cand.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
                continue
            cand /= s

            cm = float(conv_fast(cand).max())
            if not np.isfinite(cm):
                continue
            sc = 2.0 * n * cm
            if sc < best_step_score - 1e-13:
                best_step_score = sc
                best_step_arr = cand
                best_step_eta = e

        improved = False
        if best_step_arr is not None and best_step_score < current_score - 1e-13:
            a = best_step_arr
            current_score = best_step_score
            eta = best_step_eta
            patience = 0
            improved = True

            if current_score < best_s:
                best_s = current_score
                best_a = a.copy()

            # Validate with the authoritative evaluator periodically and
            # on significant surrogate improvement.
            need_exact = (it % 20 == 0) or (np.isfinite(best_es) and current_score < best_es * 0.999) or (not np.isfinite(best_es))
            if need_exact:
                ex = exact_score(a)
                if ex < best_es:
                    best_es = ex
                    best_exact = a.copy()
        else:
            patience += 1

            # If stuck, first try small random multiplicative moves.
            if patience >= 8:
                best_pert = None
                best_pert_score = current_score

                for scale_p in (0.04, 0.10, 0.22, 0.45, 0.80):
                    ap = perturb_array(a, scale_p)
                    if ap is None:
                        continue
                    sc = surrogate_score(ap)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_pert_score:
                        best_pert_score = sc
                        best_pert = ap

                accept_limit = 2.0
                if np.isfinite(best_es):
                    accept_limit = min(2.0, float(best_es) + 0.18)

                if best_pert is not None:
                    if best_pert_score < current_score - 1e-13 or (patience >= 20 and best_pert_score < accept_limit):
                        a = best_pert
                        current_score = best_pert_score
                        patience = 0
                        eta = min(3.0, max(eta, 0.6))
                        beta_mult = min(200.0, max(20.0, beta_mult * 0.75))
                        mix = min(0.25, mix + 0.04)
                        continue

            # If still stuck, blend with the initial pool and adjust
            # the smoothing parameters.
            if patience >= 25:
                a_new, sc_new, used_pool = blend_with_pool(a, current_score)
                if used_pool:
                    a = a_new
                    current_score = sc_new
                    patience = 0
                    beta_mult = 40.0
                    mix = min(0.25, max(0.05, mix))
                    eta = 0.8
                    continue

                # Reset to a smoother phase.
                beta_mult = float(np.clip(beta_mult * 1.25, 20.0, 220.0))
                mix = float(np.clip(mix + 0.03, 0.01, 0.25))
                eta = float(np.clip(eta * 1.15, 0.15, 4.0))

                if patience >= 70:
                    beta_mult = 25.0
                    mix = 0.12
                    eta = 0.9
                    patience = 0
                    a = best_a.copy()
                    current_score = surrogate_score(a)

        # Gentle annealing.
        if it % 100 == 0:
            beta_mult = float(np.clip(beta_mult * 0.98, 18.0, 220.0))
            mix = float(np.clip(mix * 0.97, 0.01, 0.25))
            eta = float(np.clip(eta * 0.99, 0.15, 4.0))

        # If we are comfortably below the displayed target, still allow a
        # little polishing but avoid spending excessive solver time.
        if np.isfinite(best_es) and best_es < 1.42 and it > 2000:
            break

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final validation and safe return.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Re-evaluate the current running point; it may be slightly better.
    if np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        ex = exact_score(a)
        if ex < best_es:
            best_es = ex
            best_exact = a.copy()

    # Re-evaluate the stored exact best in case a was modified/corrupted.
    if best_exact is not None:
        ex = exact_score(best_exact)
        if ex < best_es:
            best_es = ex

    if best_exact is None or not np.isfinite(best_es):
        fallback = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        return make_list(fallback)

    return make_list(best_exact)
```
